Present columns: {list(dock.columns)}\")\n\n    dock_loaded = dock[dock[\"Status\"].astype(str) == \"LOADED\"].copy()\n\n    # Find the best timestamp column we can sort by.\n    ts_col = None\n    for candidate in [\"Scan Timestamp\", \"Timestamp\", \"Scan Time\", \"Scanned At\"]:\n        if candidate in dock_loaded.columns:\n            ts_col = candidate\n            break\n    if ts_col is None:\n        # Fall back to row order only.\n        dock_loaded[\"_ts_dt\"] = pd.NaT\n    else:\n        dock_loaded[\"_ts_dt\"] = pd.to_datetime(dock_loaded[ts_col], errors=\"coerce\")\n\n    dock_loaded[\"_dock_row\"] = range(len(dock_loaded))\n\n    for req in [\"Shipment ID\", \"Carton ID\"]:\n        if req not in dock_loaded.columns:\n            raise KeyError(f\"Dock log missing '{req}' column. Present columns: {list(dock.columns)}\")\n\n    # Sort so the latest LOADED per (Shipment ID, Carton ID) ends up last.\n    dock_loaded = dock_loaded.sort_values(\n        by=[\"Shipment ID\", \"Carton ID\", \"_ts_dt\", \"_dock_row\"],\n        ascending=[True, True, True, True],\n        kind=\"mergesort\",  # stable\n    )\n\n    kept = dock_loaded.drop_duplicates(subset=[\"Shipment ID\", \"Carton ID\"], keep=\"last\").copy()\n\n    # Merge onto manifest, preserving manifest row order.\n    manifest[\"_manifest_row\"] = range(len(manifest))\n\n    merge_cols = [\"Shipment ID\", \"Carton ID\"]\n    for c in [\"Scanned Zone\", ts_col, \"Status\"]:\n        if c and c in kept.columns and c not in merge_cols:\n            merge_cols.append(c)\n\n    merged = manifest.merge(\n        kept[merge_cols],\n        how=\"left\",\n        on=[\"Shipment ID\", \"Carton ID\"],\n        sort=False,\n        suffixes=(\"\", \"_dock\"),\n    )\n    merged = merged.sort_values(\"_manifest_row\", kind=\"mergesort\").reset_index(drop=True)\n\n    # 1-C Compute error columns (value-only)\n    has_loaded = merged[\"Status\"].astype(str).eq(\"LOADED\")\n\n    merged[\"Missing Load Scan\"] = (~has_loaded).astype(int)\n\n    # Only evaluate mismatch when we have a LOADED scan.\n    if \"Scanned Zone\" not in merged.columns:\n        merged[\"Scanned Zone\"] = pd.NA\n\n    planned = merged[\"Planned Zone\"].astype(\"string\")\n    scanned = merged[\"Scanned Zone\"].astype(\"string\")\n\n    merged[\"Zone Mismatch\"] = (has_loaded & (scanned.notna()) & (planned.notna()) & (scanned != planned)).astype(int)\n\n    merged[\"Total Errors\"] = (merged[\"Missing Load Scan\"] + merged[\"Zone Mismatch\"]).astype(int)\n\n    def summarize(row) -> str | None:\n        parts = []\n        if int(row[\"Missing Load Scan\"]) == 1:\n            parts.append(\"Missing Load Scan\")\n        if int(row[\"Zone Mismatch\"]) == 1:\n            parts.append(\"Zone Mismatch\")\n        if not parts:\n            return None\n        return \", \".join(parts)\n\n    merged[\"Error Summary\"] = merged.apply(summarize, axis=1)\n\n    # 1-D Build Formatted Data\n    formatted = merged[REQUIRED_MANIFEST_HEADERS + [\"Missing Load Scan\", \"Zone Mismatch\", \"Total Errors\", \"Error Summary\"]].copy()\n\n    # Ensure the three numeric columns are plain ints for Excel.\n    for c in [\"Missing Load Scan\", \"Zone Mismatch\", \"Total Errors\"]:\n        formatted[c] = formatted[c].fillna(0).astype(int)\n\n    # 1-E Build Summary\n    summary = (\n        formatted.groupby([\"Route\", \"Shipment ID\"], as_index=False)[\n            [\"Missing Load Scan\", \"Zone Mismatch\", \"Total Errors\"]\n        ]\n        .sum()\n    )\n    summary = summary[summary[\"Total Errors\"] > 0].copy()\n    summary = summary.sort_values([\"Route\", \"Shipment ID\"], ascending=[True, True], kind=\"mergesort\")\n\n    summary = summary.rename(\n        columns={\n            \"Missing Load Scan\": \"Missing Load Scans\",\n            \"Zone Mismatch\": \"Zone Mismatches\",\n        }\n    )\n\n    grand = {\n        \"Route\": \"Grand Total\",\n        \"Shipment ID\": \"-\",\n        \"Missing Load Scans\": int(summary[\"Missing Load Scans\"].sum()) if len(summary) else int(formatted[\"Missing Load Scan\"].sum()),\n        \"Zone Mismatches\": int(summary[\"Zone Mismatches\"].sum()) if len(summary) else int(formatted[\"Zone Mismatch\"].sum()),\n        \"Total Errors\": int(summary[\"Total Errors\"].sum()) if len(summary) else int(formatted[\"Total Errors\"].sum()),\n    }\n\n    summary_out = pd.concat([summary[[\"Route\", \"Shipment ID\", \"Missing Load Scans\", \"Zone Mismatches\", \"Total Errors\"]], pd.DataFrame([grand])], ignore_index=True)\n\n    # 1-F Write the workbook (preserve Overview)\n    wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(TEMPLATE_PATH)\n\n    # Delete then recreate specified sheets\n    for name in [\"RawData\", \"Formatted Data\", \"Summary\"]:\n        if name in wb.sheetnames:\n            del wb[name]\n\n    # Create after Overview (keep it first)\n    overview_idx = wb.sheetnames.index(\"Overview\") if \"Overview\" in wb.sheetnames else 0\n\n    ws_raw = wb.create_sheet(\"RawData\", index=overview_idx + 1)\n    ws_fmt = wb.create_sheet(\"Formatted Data\", index=overview_idx + 2)\n    ws_sum = wb.create_sheet(\"Summary\", index=overview_idx + 3)\n\n    write_df_to_sheet(ws_raw, manifest.drop(columns=[\"_manifest_row\"], errors=\"ignore\"))\n    write_df_to_sheet(ws_fmt, formatted)\n    write_df_to_sheet(ws_sum, summary_out)\n\n    wb.save(OUT_XLSX)\n\n    # 1-G Build Word brief\n    total_missing = int(formatted[\"Missing Load Scan\"].sum())\n    total_mismatch = int(formatted[\"Zone Mismatch\"].sum())\n    total_errors = int(formatted[\"Total Errors\"].sum())\n\n    # Top shipments by errors (exclude Grand Total / '-' placeholders)\n    top_shipments = []\n    if len(summary) > 0:\n        top_shipments = (\n            summary.sort_values(\"Total Errors\", ascending=False, kind=\"mergesort\")\n            .head(5)[\"Shipment ID\"]\n            .astype(str)\n            .tolist()\n        )\n\n    # Ensure we mention at least two shipment IDs when possible.\n    if len(top_shipments) < 2:\n        alt = (\n            formatted.groupby(\"Shipment ID\", as_index=False)[\"Total Errors\"].sum()\n            .sort_values(\"Total Errors\", ascending=False, kind=\"mergesort\")\n            .head(2)[\"Shipment ID\"]\n            .astype(str)\n            .tolist()\n        )\n        for x in alt:\n            if x not in top_shipments:\n                top_shipments.append(x)\n    top_shipments = top_shipments[:2]\n\n    doc = Document()\n\n    # 3–6 sentences, plain language, include definitions + totals + recommendation + high-priority shipments\n    shipments_clause = \" and \".join(top_shipments) if top_shipments else \"the highest-error shipments\"\n\n    p = (\n        \"This outbound load audit compares the planned manifest to dock scanning activity. \"\n        \"A Missing Load Scan is a carton listed on the manifest that has no confirmed LOADED scan in the dock log, \"\n        \"and a Zone Mismatch is a carton whose scanned zone differs from its planned zone. \"\n        f\"The audit identified {total_missing} Missing Load Scans, {total_mismatch} Zone Mismatches, and {total_errors} Total Errors. \"\n        f\"The highest-priority shipments to review are {shipments_clause}. \"\n        \"Recommendation: add a pre-departure exception check that blocks trailer closeout when any carton is missing a LOADED scan or is scanned to the wrong zone, \"\n        \"and retrain loaders to re-scan cartons immediately after any zone rework.\"\n    )\n\n    doc.add_paragraph(p)\n    doc.save(OUT_DOCX)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}],"model":"openai/gpt-5.2-20251211","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"OpenAI","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8cb5371b-258f-4e1d-b55f-b4f3b89a983d","uuid":"6de44901-754a-4b60-870f-f02d2bf57681"}

Present columns: {list(dock.columns)}\")\n\n    dock_loaded = dock[dock[\"Status\"].astype(str) == \"LOADED\"].copy()\n\n    # Find the best timestamp column we can sort by.\n    ts_col = None\n    for candidate in [\"Scan Timestamp\", \"Timestamp\", \"Scan Time\", \"Scanned At\"]:\n        if candidate in dock_loaded.columns:\n            ts_col = candidate\n            break\n    if ts_col is None:\n        # Fall back to row order only.\n        dock_loaded[\"_ts_dt\"] = pd.NaT\n    else:\n        dock_loaded[\"_ts_dt\"] = pd.to_datetime(dock_loaded[ts_col], errors=\"coerce\")\n\n    dock_loaded[\"_dock_row\"] = range(len(dock_loaded))\n\n    for req in [\"Shipment ID\", \"Carton ID\"]:\n        if req not in dock_loaded.columns:\n            raise KeyError(f\"Dock log missing '{req}' column. Present columns: {list(dock.columns)}\")\n\n    # Sort so the latest LOADED per (Shipment ID, Carton ID) ends up last.\n    dock_loaded = dock_loaded.sort_values(\n        by=[\"Shipment ID\", \"Carton ID\", \"_ts_dt\", \"_dock_row\"],\n        ascending=[True, True, True, True],\n        kind=\"mergesort\",  # stable\n    )\n\n    kept = dock_loaded.drop_duplicates(subset=[\"Shipment ID\", \"Carton ID\"], keep=\"last\").copy()\n\n    # Merge onto manifest, preserving manifest row order.\n    manifest[\"_manifest_row\"] = range(len(manifest))\n\n    merge_cols = [\"Shipment ID\", \"Carton ID\"]\n    for c in [\"Scanned Zone\", ts_col, \"Status\"]:\n        if c and c in kept.columns and c not in merge_cols:\n            merge_cols.append(c)\n\n    merged = manifest.merge(\n        kept[merge_cols],\n        how=\"left\",\n        on=[\"Shipment ID\", \"Carton ID\"],\n        sort=False,\n        suffixes=(\"\", \"_dock\"),\n    )\n    merged = merged.sort_values(\"_manifest_row\", kind=\"mergesort\").reset_index(drop=True)\n\n    # 1-C Compute error columns (value-only)\n    has_loaded = merged[\"Status\"].astype(str).eq(\"LOADED\")\n\n    merged[\"Missing Load Scan\"] = (~has_loaded).astype(int)\n\n    # Only evaluate mismatch when we have a LOADED scan.\n    if \"Scanned Zone\" not in merged.columns:\n        merged[\"Scanned Zone\"] = pd.NA\n\n    planned = merged[\"Planned Zone\"].astype(\"string\")\n    scanned = merged[\"Scanned Zone\"].astype(\"string\")\n\n    merged[\"Zone Mismatch\"] = (has_loaded & (scanned.notna()) & (planned.notna()) & (scanned != planned)).astype(int)\n\n    merged[\"Total Errors\"] = (merged[\"Missing Load Scan\"] + merged[\"Zone Mismatch\"]).astype(int)\n\n    def summarize(row) -> str | None:\n        parts = []\n        if int(row[\"Missing Load Scan\"]) == 1:\n            parts.append(\"Missing Load Scan\")\n        if int(row[\"Zone Mismatch\"]) == 1:\n            parts.append(\"Zone Mismatch\")\n        if not parts:\n            return None\n        return \", \".join(parts)\n\n    merged[\"Error Summary\"] = merged.apply(summarize, axis=1)\n\n    # 1-D Build Formatted Data\n    formatted = merged[REQUIRED_MANIFEST_HEADERS + [\"Missing Load Scan\", \"Zone Mismatch\", \"Total Errors\", \"Error Summary\"]].copy()\n\n    # Ensure the three numeric columns are plain ints for Excel.\n    for c in [\"Missing Load Scan\", \"Zone Mismatch\", \"Total Errors\"]:\n        formatted[c] = formatted[c].fillna(0).astype(int)\n\n    # 1-E Build Summary\n    summary = (\n        formatted.groupby([\"Route\", \"Shipment ID\"], as_index=False)[\n            [\"Missing Load Scan\", \"Zone Mismatch\", \"Total Errors\"]\n        ]\n        .sum()\n    )\n    summary = summary[summary[\"Total Errors\"] > 0].copy()\n    summary = summary.sort_values([\"Route\", \"Shipment ID\"], ascending=[True, True], kind=\"mergesort\")\n\n    summary = summary.rename(\n        columns={\n            \"Missing Load Scan\": \"Missing Load Scans\",\n            \"Zone Mismatch\": \"Zone Mismatches\",\n        }\n    )\n\n    grand = {\n        \"Route\": \"Grand Total\",\n        \"Shipment ID\": \"-\",\n        \"Missing Load Scans\": int(summary[\"Missing Load Scans\"].sum()) if len(summary) else int(formatted[\"Missing Load Scan\"].sum()),\n        \"Zone Mismatches\": int(summary[\"Zone Mismatches\"].sum()) if len(summary) else int(formatted[\"Zone Mismatch\"].sum()),\n        \"Total Errors\": int(summary[\"Total Errors\"].sum()) if len(summary) else int(formatted[\"Total Errors\"].sum()),\n    }\n\n    summary_out = pd.concat([summary[[\"Route\", \"Shipment ID\", \"Missing Load Scans\", \"Zone Mismatches\", \"Total Errors\"]], pd.DataFrame([grand])], ignore_index=True)\n\n    # Top shipments by errors (exclude Grand Total / '-' placeholders)\n    top_shipments = []\n    if len(summary) > 0:\n        top_shipments = (\n            summary.sort_values(\"Total Errors\", ascending=False, kind=\"mergesort\")\n            .head(5)[\"Shipment ID\"]\n            .astype(str)\n            .tolist()\n        )\n\n    # 3–6 sentences, plain language, include definitions + totals + recommendation + high-priority shipments\n    shipments_clause = \" and \".join(top_shipments) if top_shipments else \"the highest-error shipments\"\n\n    p = (\n        \"This outbound load audit compares the planned manifest to dock scanning activity. \"\n        \"A Missing Load Scan is a carton listed on the manifest that has no confirmed LOADED scan in the dock log, \"\n        \"and a Zone Mismatch is a carton whose scanned zone differs from its planned zone. \"\n        f\"The audit identified {total_missing} Missing Load Scans, {total_mismatch} Zone Mismatches, and {total_errors} Total Errors. \"\n        f\"The highest-priority shipments to review are {shipments_clause}. \"\n        \"Recommendation: add a pre-departure exception check that blocks trailer closeout when any carton is missing a LOADED scan or is scanned to the wrong zone, \"\n        \"and retrain loaders to re-scan cartons immediately after any zone rework.\"\n    )\n\n    doc.add_paragraph(p)\n    doc.save(OUT_DOCX)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
